# Runtime Data Ledger rev 3 - group macros-aether

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 3](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/joltab`, `merge/ke16-into-ecsnative` @ `d11962a9`. **Rows:** 462, of which **462 active**; 116 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: out-of-scope:compile-time 457, kernel-internal 5. Active classes: K 5, C 457. Crates: aether_lang 263, boyko_macros 199. Rungs: OOS 457, R2 5.

## Contents

- [Findings](#findings)
- [Kernel features](#kernel-features)
- [Primitives refuted](#primitives-refuted)
- [Plan conflicts](#plan-conflicts)
- [Rows](#rows)

## Findings

- The 5 K rows are emitted into EVERY deriving binary and leak lazily on first use. The bundle and component rows leak on the first `static_info()` call, which can come after boot, so they must move before the G3 DenyAfterSteady gate is armed. The event rows are reached at preregister, i.e. at boot. This is reasoned, not measured.
- `get_event_participants` (event_registry.rs:272) has zero callers, so the participant datum is write-only today.

## Kernel features

The features this group proposed, with the index id they merged into.

| group name | index id | rev-3 status |
|---|---|---|
| static-type-descriptors | KF-43 | active |

## Primitives refuted

- **per-type inline static (verified new_primitives[0], "none required (preferred destination)")** - *REFUTED as a primitive, KEPT as the destination*
  - replaced by: kernel-internal
  - evidence: Not a memory primitive: it is the kernel-internal form realised in static storage, the class the kernel already uses for its process-global registries (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/events/event_registry.rs:87 "static EVENT_INFO: [OnceLock&lt;EventInfo>; MAX_EVENTS] ="; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle_type_registry.rs:93 "static BUNDLE_NEXT_ID: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);") and that the same emitted fn already declares (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:331 "static INFO: ::std::sync::OnceLock&lt;"). Nothing is added to the memory library and nothing is allocated. Sound because all 5 impls are non-generic (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:26 "if !input.generics.params.is_empty() {"; crates/boyko_macros/src/event.rs:28 "if !s.generics.params.is_empty() {"; crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:1050 "never handles generics (`input.generics` is not read anywhere in this") and all 5 lengths are macro-time constants (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:16 "const MAX_BUNDLE_ARITY: usize = 16;"; crates/boyko_macros/src/event.rs:230 "let participant_count = participant_fields.len();").
- **StaticDescriptorArena (verified new_primitives[1], FALLBACK: process-global VmReservation-backed append-only bump arena returning &'static [T])** - *REFUTED for every row of this group*
  - replaced by: kernel-internal (per-type static; event.rs:378 needs only the literal &[])
  - evidence: The arena only earns its place for a descriptor whose length is unknown at expansion or whose impl is generic; this group has neither (N &lt;= 16 per crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:16 "const MAX_BUNDLE_ARITY: usize = 16;", 1, K, and P per crates/boyko_macros/src/event.rs:230 "let participant_count = participant_fields.len();"; generics rejected or never read: crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:26 "if !input.generics.params.is_empty() {", crates/boyko_macros/src/event.rs:28 "if !s.generics.params.is_empty() {", crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:1050 "never handles generics (`input.generics` is not read anywhere in this"). Forms before kernel-internal fail per row: component / dense-component because types are not entities (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/identifiers/primitives.rs:15 "pub struct $name(pub usize);"); resource-column because the descriptor is world-free and process-global (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:435 "fn static_info() -> &'static BundleStaticInfo;"; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:452 "threads and equal across `EcsMaster` instances."); event / relation / enable-state / system-scratch / scope-arena because it is not a message, not an entity-to-entity edge, not a boolean and not transient. Whether the kernel's runtime-length leak family (kernel_features[0].cross_group) needs such an arena is an ecs-group decision; if it does, it is the registry's own kernel-internal storage, not a primitive beside ComponentPool.

## Plan conflicts

- SBC8 vs the owner's 2026-09-10 order. docs/archive/PHASE-8.5-STATIC-BUNDLE-CACHE-PLAN.md:130 "* **SBC8** — Each `Bundle::component_ids()` slice is heap-leaked exactly once per Bundle type per process via `Box::leak`." is archived, yet live code still cites it as the licence (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:339 "// Leak is bounded by SBC8 (one slice per Bundle type per"; crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:896 "sorted. Leak bounded by SBC8 (once per type per process)."; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/self_bundle.rs:64 "// bounded by SBC8 (once per type per process)."). The ledger routes bundle.rs:349 and component.rs:900 to a heap-free static. The SBC8 comments and the kernel doc contract (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:316 "it ascending by `ComponentId.0` (canonical order, **B1**), and leaks"; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:351 "component-id slice. Leaked exactly once per Bundle type per process") must be reworded in the same change, or they will describe code no longer emitted.
- ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE `offline` tag vs emitted runtime code. D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:50 "\| `offline` \| `boyko_macros` (proc-macro), `aether_lang`, `boyko_shaderdsl` printer, `boyko_fontbake` \| compile/bake time \| not in the runtime binary or run once at bake \|" files boyko_macros as not in the runtime binary. True for the 457 C rows, false for the 5 K rows: their code is emitted into every binary that derives Bundle/Component or uses #[event] and leaks lazily on first use. Read literally, the tag would exempt them while G3 (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:58 "2. *Structural*: `DenyAfterSteady`") aborts on a post-steady first touch. rg "leak\|descriptor\|static_info\|per-type static" over that file finds no row for the once-per-type Box::leak family (only Miri leak-flag and Heap back-pointer lines, :371 and :439).
- No disagreement between sibling plans on the event rows: docs/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md:1861 "**RESOLVED 2026-08-28 — reading 1, scoped to the machine event router (owner delegated the call).**" and docs/aether-v2/DECISIONS.md:259 "(`entity(EnemyBrain)`) becomes a **debug_assert in the router**" keep the participant datum with a debug-only reader; the static destination serves it. NOTE: that reader does not exist at ca582e72 (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/events/event_registry.rs:272 "pub fn get_event_participants(event_id: EventId) -> Option&lt;&'static [ParticipantInfo]> {" has zero callers; required_components has no reader), so the datum is write-only in this tree. DENSE-COMPONENTS-PLAN, ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION, DENSE-ENABLE-QUERY-PLAN, OPTIMIZATION-PLAN-PHYSICS, RENDER-PHYSICS-GPU-PLAN and physics/FMA-DETERMINISM contain no Box::leak/static_info/participant/boyko_macros mention (rg at ca582e72); POOL-SUBGRANULAR-PACKING-PLAN.md is absent at ca582e72; ADVANCED-PHYSICS-DESIGN-SPACE mentions Aether only as a machine surface (:697), not these rows.

- **owner**, **container&lt;elem&gt;**, **class**, **ecs_form**, **owning_entity**

- **plan_ref**
- **note:** `[kind; growth; addr_cached; rung]`, then `ECS:` with the form rationale (one sentence per skipped earlier form, then any `[rev2 ...]` blocks), then `Class:` with the inventory/recount note, then `Readers:`.

| crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:349 `] = ::std::boxed::Box::leak(::std::boxed::Box::new(arr));` | &lt;T: #[derive(Bundle)]>::static_info -> BundleStaticInfo::component_ids (emitted by boyko_macros::bundle::expand, quote! at bundle.rs:320) | `Box<T><[ComponentId; N] (N = #[derive(Bundle)] field count, <= MAX_BUNDLE_ARITY = 16; bundle.rs:16/98) -> leaked to &'static>` | K | kernel-internal | none | NEW:per-type inline static -- emit `static IDS: ::std::sync::OnceLock<[ComponentId; N]>` beside INFO and hand out `IDS.get_or_init(..).as_slice()`; no heap and no library primitive (N is a macro-time constant; generics are rejected at bundle.rs:26 so the static is per type). Fallback if a heap-free static is refused: a StaticDescriptorArena (see new_primitives) | SBC8 (ARCHIVED, still cited as the licence by live code): docs/archive/PHASE-8.5-STATIC-BUNDLE-CACHE-PLAN.md:130 "* **SBC8** — Each `Bundle::component_ids()` slice is heap-leaked exactly once per Bundle type per process via `Box::leak`." It decided the heap leak is acceptable; the owner's 2026-09-10 order supersedes it - flagged in plan_conflicts. | [return; growth once; addr_cached yes; rung R2] ECS: ecs_form kernel-internal: the Bundle type-registry entry (canonical sorted component-id set), read on every spawn/insert: crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/migration_helpers.rs:221 "let bundle_ids = B::component_ids();", crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/migration_helpers.rs:433 "let has_requires = component_registry::any_requires(B::component_ids());", crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle_column_cache.rs:278 "let component_ids = B::component_ids();", crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/spawn_batch_command.rs:328 "for the dense path rides `B::component_ids()`". Destination: emit `static IDS: OnceLock<[ComponentId; N]>` beside the existing per-type INFO static (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:331 "static INFO: ::std::sync::OnceLock&lt;"); INFO's init sorts inside IDS.get_or_init and stores `.as_slice()`. N is a macro-time constant &lt;= 16 (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:16 "const MAX_BUNDLE_ARITY: usize = 16;") and generics are rejected (crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:26 "if !input.generics.params.is_empty() {"), so one static per type is sound. ComponentId is minted at runtime, which rules out a const array but not a OnceLock static. Kernel twin: NONE for the N-field derive (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/app/app.rs:1253 "let leaked: &'static [ComponentId; 1] = Box::leak(Box::new(arr));" sits in the test module opened at crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/app/app.rs:915 "#[cfg(test)]"). Doc contract to reword in lock-step (ecs group): crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:316 "it ascending by `ComponentId.0` (canonical order, **B1**), and leaks"; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:351 "component-id slice. Leaked exactly once per Bundle type per process"; and the macro comment crates/boyko_macros/src/bundle.rs:339 "// Leak is bounded by SBC8 (one slice per Bundle type per". ECS forms tried in order. COMPONENT: refuted. The datum describes a Rust TYPE, not an entity, and types are not entities in this kernel: ComponentId is a bare integer (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/identifiers/primitives.rs:15 "pub struct $name(pub usize);", minted for crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/identifiers/primitives.rs:61 "ComponentId);"); rg over boyko_ecs/src and docs/ for "type entit\|component entit\|ComponentEntity" finds no type-as-entity concept. DENSE-COMPONENT: refuted for the same reason (no entity row to key it by); in addition the datum is process-global while every ECS store is per EcsMaster (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:452 "threads and equal across `EcsMaster` instances.") and the trait accessor takes no world (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:435 "fn static_info() -> &'static BundleStaticInfo;"). ENABLE-STATE: refuted, not a boolean. RELATION: refuted, a type-to-component-type edge needs type entities, which do not exist. EVENT: refuted, never produced or consumed as a message; written once per process. RESOURCE-COLUMN: refuted. Resources are per world (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/ecs_master/ecs_master.rs:102 "pub(crate) resources: Resources,"), but readers reach the datum through the world-free trait (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:444 "Self::static_info().component_ids"); a per-world copy would duplicate the table per EcsMaster and break the &'static contract the kernel stores. SYSTEM-SCRATCH / SCOPE-ARENA: refuted, not transient. KERNEL-INTERNAL: FITS. It is the kernel's own type-registration metadata, the same class as the process-global registries that already live in static storage, not on any heap (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/events/event_registry.rs:87 "static EVENT_INFO: [OnceLock&lt;EventInfo>; MAX_EVENTS] ="; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle_type_registry.rs:93 "static BUNDLE_NEXT_ID: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);"). The destination is a per-type static sized at expansion: nothing is allocated, so nothing needs VmReservation, and the G5 note already accepts std OnceLock in boyko_ecs (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:311 "Not for `boyko_ecs` (needs `std::sync::OnceLock`"). CONSIDERED AND NOT ADOPTED - new-kernel-feature:type-entities (flecs-style: register each component/bundle/event type as an entity and store the descriptor as a component on it). kernel-internal fits first in the closed order, so the order stops there; on the merits it would also put a per-world entity lookup on the spawn hot path in place of one Acquire load on a static, and duplicate a process-global datum per EcsMaster. Steady-state hazard (reasoned, NOT measured): the leak runs lazily inside OnceLock::get_or_init on the first static_info() call for the type, which is the first spawn/insert of that bundle type and can fall after boot. The design's G3 gate (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:58 "2. *Structural*: `DenyAfterSteady`") aborts on any heap call after enter_steady(), so these two rows must be migrated before G3 is armed in a binary that can first-touch a bundle type in the steady window. Class: RUNTIME code: this is inside a quote! body (bundle.rs:320-...), i.e. it runs in every binary that derives Bundle. One heap cell of N*8 B per Bundle type per process, lazily inside INFO.get_or_init (first static_info() call -- may be mid-frame, but at most once per type), never freed (Box::leak). Address-stable requirement is real (&'static stored in the descriptor), which is why a plain Vec/VmColumn that can relocate is NOT a valid destination; the inline static satisfies it at zero cost. RECOUNT: no hand-written RUNTIME twin of the N-field emission exists -- `rg Box::leak` over boyko_ecs/src finds self_bundle.rs:68 (the twin of the 1-element component.rs:900 emission, not of this one) and app.rs:1253 "let leaked: &'static [ComponentId; 1] = Box::leak(Box::new(arr));", which is inside `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` (app.rs:915 "#[cfg(test)]") -- a test fixture, not a runtime twin. CLASS K, not B: the lifetime is once-per-type (B-like) but the slice is a KERNEL REGISTRY ENTRY (bundle type registry / event registry) that the kernel reads after init -- B requires never touched per frame, and the bundle id slice is read on the spawn path (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/spawn_batch_command.rs:328 "for the dense path rides `B::component_ids()`"). Readers: Bundle::component_ids default impl (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:444 "Self::static_info().component_ids") and every spawn/insert-bundle path that reads the canonical id slice; the &'static slice is stored forever in BundleStaticInfo.component_ids |

| crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:900 `] = ::std::boxed::Box::leak(::std::boxed::Box::new([` | &lt;T: #[derive(Component)]>::static_info (self-bundle; emitted by boyko_macros::component::component_self_bundle_codegen, quote! at component.rs:873) | `Box<T><[ComponentId; 1] -> leaked to &'static>` | K | kernel-internal | none | NEW:per-type inline static -- `static IDS: ::std::sync::OnceLock<[ComponentId; 1]>` (or a single static ComponentId + core::slice::from_ref); no heap, no library primitive | SBC8 (ARCHIVED): docs/archive/PHASE-8.5-STATIC-BUNDLE-CACHE-PLAN.md:130 "* **SBC8** — Each `Bundle::component_ids()` slice is heap-leaked exactly once per Bundle type per process via `Box::leak`." Cited by the emission itself: crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:896 "sorted. Leak bounded by SBC8 (once per type per process).". Superseded by the owner's 2026-09-10 order - flagged in plan_conflicts. | [return; growth once; addr_cached yes; rung R2] ECS: ecs_form kernel-internal: the self-bundle registry entry of a spawnable component (1-element id set), emitted unless no_bundle / bitset / dense (crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:334 "let bundle_items = if hooks.no_bundle \|\| hooks.storage_bitset \|\| hooks.storage_dense {"). Destination: `static ID: OnceLock<ComponentId>` handed out through core::slice::from_ref, or `static IDS: OnceLock<[ComponentId; 1]>`. The derive never reads generics (crates/boyko_macros/src/component.rs:1050 "never handles generics (`input.generics` is not read anywhere in this"), so the emitted impl is always non-generic and one static per type is sound. Hand-written runtime twin that must move in lock-step (ecs-group row): crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/self_bundle.rs:68 "] = ::std::boxed::Box::leak(::std::boxed::Box::new([" with its SBC8 comment crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/self_bundle.rs:64 "// bounded by SBC8 (once per type per process).". Every Aether `component` reaches this site through crates/aether_lang/src/expand.rs:189 "#[derive(::boyko_macros::Component)]". ECS forms tried in order. COMPONENT: refuted. The datum describes a Rust TYPE, not an entity, and types are not entities in this kernel: ComponentId is a bare integer (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/identifiers/primitives.rs:15 "pub struct $name(pub usize);", minted for crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/identifiers/primitives.rs:61 "ComponentId);"); rg over boyko_ecs/src and docs/ for "type entit\|component entit\|ComponentEntity" finds no type-as-entity concept. DENSE-COMPONENT: refuted for the same reason (no entity row to key it by); in addition the datum is process-global while every ECS store is per EcsMaster (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:452 "threads and equal across `EcsMaster` instances.") and the trait accessor takes no world (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:435 "fn static_info() -> &'static BundleStaticInfo;"). ENABLE-STATE: refuted, not a boolean. RELATION: refuted, a type-to-component-type edge needs type entities, which do not exist. EVENT: refuted, never produced or consumed as a message; written once per process. RESOURCE-COLUMN: refuted. Resources are per world (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/ecs_master/ecs_master.rs:102 "pub(crate) resources: Resources,"), but readers reach the datum through the world-free trait (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:444 "Self::static_info().component_ids"); a per-world copy would duplicate the table per EcsMaster and break the &'static contract the kernel stores. SYSTEM-SCRATCH / SCOPE-ARENA: refuted, not transient. KERNEL-INTERNAL: FITS. It is the kernel's own type-registration metadata, the same class as the process-global registries that already live in static storage, not on any heap (crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/events/event_registry.rs:87 "static EVENT_INFO: [OnceLock&lt;EventInfo>; MAX_EVENTS] ="; crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle_type_registry.rs:93 "static BUNDLE_NEXT_ID: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);"). The destination is a per-type static sized at expansion: nothing is allocated, so nothing needs VmReservation, and the G5 note already accepts std OnceLock in boyko_ecs (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:311 "Not for `boyko_ecs` (needs `std::sync::OnceLock`"). CONSIDERED AND NOT ADOPTED - new-kernel-feature:type-entities (flecs-style: register each component/bundle/event type as an entity and store the descriptor as a component on it). kernel-internal fits first in the closed order, so the order stops there; on the merits it would also put a per-world entity lookup on the spawn hot path in place of one Acquire load on a static, and duplicate a process-global datum per EcsMaster. Steady-state hazard (reasoned, NOT measured): the leak runs lazily inside OnceLock::get_or_init on the first static_info() call for the type, which is the first spawn/insert of that bundle type and can fall after boot. The design's G3 gate (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:58 "2. *Structural*: `DenyAfterSteady`") aborts on any heap call after enter_steady(), so these two rows must be migrated before G3 is armed in a binary that can first-touch a bundle type in the steady window. Class: RUNTIME code in every #[derive(Component)] that is spawnable: emitted unless no_bundle / storage="bitset" / storage="dense" (component.rs:334 "let bundle_items = if hooks.no_bundle \|\| hooks.storage_bitset \|\| hooks.storage_dense {"). 8 B leaked per component type per process, once, inside OnceLock::get_or_init. Every Aether `component` construct reaches this site too (aether emits #[derive(::boyko_macros::Component)]). Hand-written runtime mirror: boyko_ecs impl_self_bundle! at self_bundle.rs:68 "] = ::std::boxed::Box::leak(::std::boxed::Box::new([" (the inventory cited :57, which is "fn static_info()") -- ecs group, must change in lock-step. Per-type static is sound here too: the derive never reads generics (component.rs:1050 "never handles generics (`input.generics` is not read anywhere in this"), so a generic type cannot get this impl at all. CLASS K, not B: the lifetime is once-per-type (B-like) but the slice is a KERNEL REGISTRY ENTRY (bundle type registry / event registry) that the kernel reads after init -- B requires never touched per frame, and the bundle id slice is read on the spawn path (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/commands/spawn_batch_command.rs:328 "for the dense path rides `B::component_ids()`"). Readers: Bundle::component_ids / bundle_type_id default impls (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/bundle/bundle.rs:444, :455) when a bare component is spawned/inserted as a one-component bundle |
